  functions:
    - resolveWebCornerAssetDilation line 54 [export function]
      comment: Resolve one discrete 1-4 thickness into a bounded underlay recipe.
    - createWebCornerAssetDilationFilter line 89 [export function]
      comment: Create a built-in PIXI blur filter for the thickness underlay. The export name is retained for compatibility with the existing corner and span texture services. No custom Filter, GlProgram, fragment source, or module-authored shader is created here.
    - resolveWebAssetThicknessUnderlayAlpha line 214 [export function]
      comment: Resolve the alpha applied to the softened underlay display object. The crisp source remains at full presentation alpha. Keeping the underlay below half opacity prevents the built-in blur from whitening the fibre core while still increasing its apparent width.
  constants:
    - WEB_CORNER_ASSET_DILATION_LEVELS line 12 [export const]
      comment: PIXI-version-tolerant thickness underlay for corner and span WebP assets. The former implementation used a custom fragment shader. Foundry defers custom shader compilation until the first filtered display is rendered, so an otherwise successful module load could still fail later with PixiJS' generic "Could not initialize shader" error. This implementation uses only PIXI's built-in BlurFilter. A crisp unfiltered sprite or mesh remains above the softened underlay, producing a controlled bloom-like widening without directional offset copies or module-authored GLSL.
  classes:
    - <none>
  named exports/re-exports:
    - <none>

    - createWallJunctionTopology line 24 [export function]
      comment: Build a deterministic, transient junction graph from independent Foundry wall segments. Existing endpoint nodes are seeded with the same quantised endpoint model historically used by WallCornerWebGenerator. A uniform-grid broad phase then discovers endpoint-on-segment contacts and interior wall crossings. Each accepted contact becomes a transient junction with one outward ray for every wall direction leaving that point. Collinear overlaps remain excluded. @param {object} [options={}] - Topology inputs. @param {ReadonlyArray<object>} [options.wallSegments=[]] - Plain walls. @param {number} [options.endpointTolerancePx=4] - Contact tolerance. @param {number} [options.gridSize=100] - Scene grid size in pixels. @param {number|null} [options.cellSizePx=null] - Optional broad-phase cell. @returns {Readonly<object>} Frozen junctions and diagnostics.

17. Parser limitations
----------------------
- This is a regex/static scan, not a full JavaScript AST parse.
- Dynamic imports and computed paths may be incomplete.
- Dynamic settings and flags are displayed explicitly as <dynamic: ...> rather than guessed.
- Low-confidence conventional template pairings are useful hints, not proof of runtime use.
- Function call detection is name-based and approximate.
- Nested functions, destructuring exports, and complex declarations may be partially reported.
- Unreachable files are only unreachable from module.json static imports; they may still be used by macros, globals, hooks, or dynamic runtime logic.
